<!-- Chi su dung script nay, khong tai tour-live-search.js -->

<script>
(function () {
    "use strict";

    function initializeHappyTripLiveSearch() {
        const inputs = Array.from(
            document.querySelectorAll(
                "#happytripHeroSearch, #diadiemInputTop, #diadiemInput"
            )
        );

        const resultBox =
            document.getElementById("happytripSearchResults");

        if (!inputs.length || !resultBox) {
            return;
        }

        let currentInput = null;
        let timer = null;
        let abortController = null;
        let requestId = 0;

        inputs.forEach(input => {
            input.setAttribute("autocomplete", "off");
            input.setAttribute("aria-autocomplete", "list");
            input.setAttribute(
                "aria-controls",
                "happytripSearchResults"
            );
        });

        function positionResults() {
            if (!currentInput ||
                resultBox.style.display === "none") {
                return;
            }

            const rect = currentInput.getBoundingClientRect();

            const viewportWidth = window.innerWidth;
            const viewportHeight = window.innerHeight;

            const width = Math.min(
                Math.max(rect.width, 310),
                viewportWidth - 20
            );

            const left = Math.max(
                10,
                Math.min(
                    rect.left,
                    viewportWidth - width - 10
                )
            );

            const below = viewportHeight - rect.bottom;
            const openUpwards =
                below < 210 && rect.top > 230;

            resultBox.style.left = `${left}px`;
            resultBox.style.width = `${width}px`;

            if (openUpwards) {
                resultBox.style.top = "auto";
                resultBox.style.bottom =
                    `${viewportHeight - rect.top + 6}px`;

                resultBox.style.maxHeight =
                    `${Math.max(130, rect.top - 15)}px`;
            } else {
                resultBox.style.bottom = "auto";
                resultBox.style.top =
                    `${rect.bottom + 6}px`;

                resultBox.style.maxHeight =
                    `${Math.max(130, below - 15)}px`;
            }
        }

        function showResults() {
            resultBox.style.display = "block";
            positionResults();
        }

        function hideResults() {
            resultBox.style.display = "none";
        }

        function showMessage(message) {
            resultBox.replaceChildren();

            const item = document.createElement("div");
            item.className = "ht-search-message";
            item.textContent = message;

            resultBox.appendChild(item);
            showResults();
        }

        function renderResults(tours) {
            resultBox.replaceChildren();

            if (!Array.isArray(tours) || tours.length === 0) {
                showMessage("Không tìm thấy tour phù hợp.");
                return;
            }

            tours.forEach(tour => {
                const link = document.createElement("a");
                link.className = "ht-search-result";

                link.href = tour.url ||
                    `/Tour/ChiTiet?id=${encodeURIComponent(tour.idTour)}`;

                const imageWrap = document.createElement("span");
                imageWrap.className = "ht-search-result__icon";

                const image = document.createElement("img");
                image.src = tour.hinhAnh || "";
                image.alt = tour.tenTour || "Hình ảnh tour";
                image.loading = "lazy";

                image.onerror = function () {
                    this.onerror = null;
                    this.remove();

                    const fallback = document.createElement("i");
                    fallback.className = "fa-solid fa-image";

                    imageWrap.appendChild(fallback);
                };

                if (tour.hinhAnh) {
                    imageWrap.appendChild(image);
                } else {
                    const fallback = document.createElement("i");
                    fallback.className = "fa-solid fa-image";

                    imageWrap.appendChild(fallback);
                }

                const info = document.createElement("span");
                info.className = "ht-search-result__info";

                const title = document.createElement("strong");
                title.textContent =
                    tour.tenTour || "Tour du lịch";

                const destination = document.createElement("small");
                destination.textContent =
                    tour.diaDiem || "Chưa cập nhật điểm đến";

                info.appendChild(title);
                info.appendChild(destination);

                const price = document.createElement("span");
                price.className = "ht-search-result__price";
                price.textContent = tour.gia || "Liên hệ";

                link.appendChild(imageWrap);
                link.appendChild(info);
                link.appendChild(price);

                resultBox.appendChild(link);
            });

            const footer = document.createElement("div");
            footer.className = "ht-search-footer";
            footer.textContent =
                `${tours.length} gợi ý • Chọn tour để xem chi tiết`;

            resultBox.appendChild(footer);
            showResults();
        }

        async function fetchTours(input, keyword, sequence) {
            const controller = new AbortController();
            abortController = controller;

            try {
                const response = await fetch(
                    "/Tour/GoiYTour?keyword=" +
                    encodeURIComponent(keyword),
                    {
                        method: "GET",
                        signal: controller.signal,
                        cache: "no-store",
                        headers: {
                            "Accept": "application/json"
                        }
                    }
                );

                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
                }

                const tours = await response.json();

                if (
                    sequence !== requestId ||
                    currentInput !== input ||
                    input.value.trim() !== keyword
                ) {
                    return;
                }

                renderResults(tours);
            } catch (error) {
                if (error.name === "AbortError") {
                    return;
                }

                console.error(
                    "[HappyTrip] Lỗi tìm kiếm tour:",
                    error
                );

                if (sequence === requestId) {
                    showMessage(
                        "Không tải được gợi ý. Vui lòng thử lại."
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        function handleSearchInput(input) {
            currentInput = input;

            clearTimeout(timer);
            abortController?.abort();

            const keyword = input.value.trim();
            const sequence = ++requestId;

            if (!keyword) {
                hideResults();
                return;
            }

            showMessage("Đang tìm tour phù hợp...");

            timer = setTimeout(function () {
                fetchTours(input, keyword, sequence);
            }, 120);
        }

        inputs.forEach(input => {
            input.addEventListener("input", function () {
                handleSearchInput(input);
            });

            input.addEventListener("focus", function () {
                currentInput = input;

                if (input.value.trim()) {
                    handleSearchInput(input);
                }
            });

            input.addEventListener("keydown", function (event) {
                if (event.key === "Escape") {
                    hideResults();
                }
            });
        });

        document.addEventListener("pointerdown", function (event) {
            const clickedInput = inputs.includes(event.target);

            const clickedResults =
                resultBox.contains(event.target);

            if (!clickedInput && !clickedResults) {
                hideResults();
            }
        });

        window.addEventListener(
            "resize",
            positionResults
        );

        window.addEventListener(
            "scroll",
            positionResults,
            true
        );
    }

    if (document.readyState === "loading") {
        document.addEventListener(
            "DOMContentLoaded",
            initializeHappyTripLiveSearch,
            { once: true }
        );
    } else {
        initializeHappyTripLiveSearch();
    }
})();
</script>


========== \Views\Tour\TheoLoai.cshtml ==========

    var lichGanNhat = ViewBag.LichGanNhat as Dictionary<int, TourWebApp.Data.Models.LichKhoiHanh>;
}

<div class="container mt-5">

    <h2 class="text-center mb-4 text-uppercase fw-bold">@title</h2>

    @if (Model == null || !Model.Any())
    {
        <div class="alert alert-warning text-center">
            Không có tour nào thuộc loại này.
        </div>
    }
    else
    {
        <div class="row g-4">
        @{
            var tourIndex = 0;
        }

        @foreach (var t in Model)
        {
            var lich = (lichGanNhat != null && lichGanNhat.ContainsKey(t.IdTour))
                ? lichGanNhat[t.IdTour]
                : null;

            string ngay = "--";
            string gio = "--";
            string socho = "--";
            string trangThai = "Không có lịch";

            if (lich != null)
            {
                ngay = lich.NgayKhoiHanh.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy");
                gio = lich.GioKhoiHanh?.ToString("HH:mm") ?? "--";
                socho = lich.SoChoConLai.ToString();
                trangThai = lich.SoChoConLai <= 0 ? "Hết chỗ" : lich.SoChoConLai <= 5 ? "Sắp hết chỗ" : "Còn chỗ";
            }

            <div class="col-md-4 d-flex reveal" style="--stagger:@(tourIndex % 8)">

                <article class="tour-card-modern tour-list-card shadow-lg rounded-4 overflow-hidden h-100 w-100 d-flex flex-column">

                    <!-- BADGE GIẢM GIÁ -->
                    @if (t.PhanTramGiam > 0)
                    {
                        <div class="position-absolute top-0 end-0 m-3 px-3 py-2 bg-warning rounded-pill text-white fw-bold">
                            -@t.PhanTramGiam%
                        </div>
                    }

                    <!-- IMAGE -->
                    <div class="position-relative tour-list-image-wrap" style="height:240px; overflow:hidden;">
                        <img src="@TourImagePathHelper.ToTourImageSrc(t.HinhAnh)"
                             class="w-100 h-100 tour-list-image"
                             style="object-fit:cover;" />
                    </div>

                    <!-- INFO OVERLAY TRỰC TIẾP -->
                    <div class="bg-light p-3 border-bottom tour-list-meta">

                        <p class="mb-1"><b>Mã tour:</b> @t.MaTour</p>
                        <p class="mb-1"><b>Điểm đến:</b> @t.DiaDiem</p>
                        <p class="mb-1"><b>Ngày gần nhất:</b> @ngay</p>
                        <p class="mb-1"><b>Giờ khởi hành:</b> @gio</p>
                        <p class="mb-1"><b>Còn lại:</b> @socho chỗ</p>
                        <p class="mb-1"><b>Trạng thái:</b> @trangThai</p>
                        <p class="mb-0"><b>Phương tiện:</b> @t.PhuongTien</p>

                    </div>

                    <!-- BODY -->
                    <div class="p-3 d-flex flex-column gap-2 flex-grow-1">

                        <h4 class="fw-bold tour-list-title">@t.TenTour</h4>

                        <div class="text-secondary mb-2">
                            @t.ThoiGian
                        </div>

                        <div class="d-flex align-items-center gap-3 mb-3">
                            <span class="fw-bold text-danger fs-4">@string.Format("{0:N0} đ", t.GiaKhuyenMai)</span>
                            <span class="text-decoration-line-through text-muted">@string.Format("{0:N0} đ", t.GiaGoc)</span>
                        </div>

                        <a asp-controller="Tour"
                           asp-action="ChiTiet"
                           asp-route-id="@t.IdTour"
                           class="btn btn-primary w-100 fw-bold rounded-pill mt-auto">
                            Xem chi tiết
                        </a>

                    </div>

                </article>

            </div>
            tourIndex++;
        }

        </div>
    }
</div>


========== \Views\Tour\_LoaiTourMenu.cshtml ==========
@model IEnumerable<TourWebApp.Data.Models.LoaiTour>

@{
    // Lấy id loại tour hiện tại (nếu đang ở trang theo loại)
    int activeId = 0;
    if (Context.Request.RouteValues["action"]?.ToString() == "TheoLoai")
    {
        int.TryParse(Context.Request.RouteValues["id"]?.ToString(), out activeId);
    }
}

<ul class="dropdown-menu shadow border-0 rounded-4 py-2">

    @foreach (var loai in Model)
    {
        bool isActive = loai.IdLoaiTour == activeId;

        <li>
            <a class="dropdown-item d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center 
                       @(isActive ? "text-primary fw-bold" : "")"
               asp-controller="Tour"
               asp-action="TheoLoai"
               asp-route-id="@loai.IdLoaiTour">

                @loai.TenLoai

                <!-- Icon mũi tên -->
                <span style="font-size: 14px; opacity: .7;">›</span>
            </a>
        </li>

        <!-- Gạch phân cách -->
        <li><hr class="dropdown-divider"></li>
    }
</ul>

        </div>

                {
                    var activeClass = string.Equals(filter, Model.SelectedFilter, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? "chip-active" : "";
                    <a class="filter-chip @activeClass" href="/destinations?filter=@filter">@filter</a>
                }
            </div>

                    {
                        <partial name="~/Views/Shared/TravelComponents/_DestinationCard.cshtml" model="item" />
                    }
                </div>
            }
        </div>
    </section>
</main>

        .ToList()
        ?? new List<TourWebApp.Data.Models.WishlistTour>();

    var soTour = danhSach.Count;

    var soTourText = soTour == 1
        ? "1 hành trình"
        : $"{soTour} hành trình";
}

                            </option>

                        </select>

                        <div class="wishlist-view-switch"
                             role="group"
                             aria-label="Chế độ hiển thị">

                            <button type="button"
                                    id="wishlistGridBtn"
                                    class="wishlist-view-btn is-active"
                                    aria-label="Hiển thị dạng lưới"
                                    aria-pressed="true">

                                <i class="fa-solid fa-table-cells-large"></i>

                            </button>

                            <button type="button"
                                    id="wishlistListBtn"
                                    class="wishlist-view-btn"
                                    aria-label="Hiển thị dạng danh sách"
                                    aria-pressed="false">

                                <i class="fa-solid fa-list"></i>

                            </button>

                        </div>

                    </div>
                }

            </div>

            <!-- EMPTY STATE -->

            @if (soTour == 0)
            {
                <div class="wishlist-empty">

                    <div class="wishlist-empty-icon">
                        <i class="fa-regular fa-heart"></i>
                    </div>

                    <h3>Chưa có hành trình yêu thích</h3>

                    <p>
                        Bạn chưa lưu tour nào.
                        Hãy khám phá HappyTrip và đánh dấu
                        những chuyến đi khiến bạn muốn xách ba lô lên đường.
                    </p>

                    <a href="/tours">

                        Khám phá tour

                        <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-right"></i>

                    </a>

                </div>
            }
            else
            {
                <!-- TOUR GRID -->

                <div id="wishlistTourGrid"
                     class="wishlist-tour-grid">

                    @for (var i = 0; i < danhSach.Count; i++)
                    {
                        var item = danhSach[i];
                        var tour = item.IdTourNavigation;

                        if (tour == null)
                        {
                            continue;
                        }

                        var gia = tour.GiaKhuyenMai ?? 0;

                        <article class="wishlist-tour-card"
                                 data-index="@i"
                                 data-name="@tour.TenTour"
                                 data-price="@gia.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)">

                            <!-- IMAGE -->

                            <div class="wishlist-card-media">

                                <img src="@TourImagePathHelper.ToTourImageSrc(tour.HinhAnh)"
                                     alt="@tour.TenTour"
                                     loading="lazy" />

                                <span class="wishlist-location-chip">

                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-location-dot"></i>

                                    @tour.DiaDiem

                                </span>

                                <!-- REMOVE VIA HEART -->

                                <form asp-controller="Wishlist"
                                      asp-action="Remove"
                                      method="post"
                                      class="wishlist-heart-form">

                                    @Html.AntiForgeryToken()

                                    <input type="hidden"
                                           name="idWishlist"
                                           value="@item.IdWishlist" />

                                    <button type="submit"
                                            class="wishlist-heart-btn"
                                            title="Bỏ yêu thích"
                                            aria-label="Bỏ yêu thích tour @tour.TenTour">

                                        <i class="fa-solid fa-heart"></i>

                                    </button>

                                </form>

                            </div>

                            <!-- CONTENT -->

                            <div class="wishlist-card-body">

                                <h3 class="wishlist-card-title">

                                    <a href="/Tour/ChiTiet/@item.IdTour">
                                        @tour.TenTour
                                    </a>

                                </h3>

                                <p class="wishlist-card-location">

                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-location-dot"></i>

                                    @tour.DiaDiem

                                </p>

                                <p class="wishlist-card-price">
                                    @string.Format("{0:N0} đ", gia)
                                </p>

                                <div class="wishlist-card-actions">

                                    <a href="/Tour/ChiTiet/@item.IdTour"
                                       class="wishlist-card-detail-btn">

                                        Xem chi tiết

                                        <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-right"></i>

                                    </a>

                                    <form asp-controller="Wishlist"
                                          asp-action="Remove"
                                          method="post"
                                          class="wishlist-card-remove-form">

                                        @Html.AntiForgeryToken()

                                        <input type="hidden"
                                               name="idWishlist"
                                               value="@item.IdWishlist" />

                                        <button type="submit"
                                                class="wishlist-card-remove-btn">

                                            <i class="fa-regular fa-trash-can"></i>

                                            Xóa

                                        </button>

                                    </form>

                                </div>

                            </div>

                        </article>
                    }

                </div>
            }

        </div>

    </main>

</div>

<script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

        const grid = document.getElementById("wishlistTourGrid");
        const sort = document.getElementById("wishlistSort");

        const gridButton = document.getElementById("wishlistGridBtn");
        const listButton = document.getElementById("wishlistListBtn");

        const decor = document.getElementById("wishlistFlightDecor");

        if (!grid) return;

        const originalCards = Array.from(
            grid.querySelectorAll(".wishlist-tour-card")
        );

        // ========================================
        // SORT
        // ========================================

        if (sort) {
            sort.addEventListener("change", function () {

                const cards = [...originalCards];
                const mode = sort.value;

                cards.sort(function (a, b) {

                    const priceA =
                        Number(a.dataset.price || 0);

                    const priceB =
                        Number(b.dataset.price || 0);

                    const nameA =
                        a.dataset.name || "";

                    const nameB =
                        b.dataset.name || "";

                    if (mode === "price-asc") {
                        return priceA - priceB;
                    }

                    if (mode === "price-desc") {
                        return priceB - priceA;
                    }

                    if (mode === "name") {
                        return nameA.localeCompare(
                            nameB,
                            "vi",
                            { sensitivity: "base" }
                        );
                    }

                    return Number(a.dataset.index) -
                           Number(b.dataset.index);

                });

                cards.forEach(function (card) {
                    grid.appendChild(card);
                });

            });
        }

        // ========================================
        // GRID / LIST
        // ========================================

        function setView(mode) {

            const isList = mode === "list";

            grid.classList.toggle("is-list", isList);

            if (gridButton) {
                gridButton.classList.toggle(
                    "is-active",
                    !isList
                );

                gridButton.setAttribute(
                    "aria-pressed",
                    String(!isList)
                );
            }

            if (listButton) {
                listButton.classList.toggle(
                    "is-active",
                    isList
                );

                listButton.setAttribute(
                    "aria-pressed",
                    String(isList)
                );
            }

            if (decor) {
                decor.classList.toggle(
                    "is-hidden",
                    isList || originalCards.length > 2
                );
            }
        }

        gridButton?.addEventListener(
            "click",
            function () {
                setView("grid");
            }
        );

        listButton?.addEventListener(
            "click",
            function () {
                setView("list");
            }
        );

    });
</script>
